*Yuanjie Lyu,Chengyu Wang,Haonan Zheng,Yuanhao Yue,Junbing Yan,Ming Wang,Jun Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出AgenticQwen系列小模型，通过多轮强化学习结合推理RL与智能体RL，利用双数据飞轮自动生成递增难度的任务，在公开基准和工业智能体系统中接近大模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业应用需要语言模型作为智能体执行多步推理和工具使用，但受限于成本和延迟，小模型更具实用性，现有小模型在智能体任务上性能不足。

Method: 采用多轮强化学习框架，结合推理RL和智能体RL；设计双数据飞轮：推理飞轮通过从错误中学习增加任务难度，智能体飞轮将线性工作流扩展为多分支行为树。使用合成数据和少量开源数据训练。

Result: 在多个智能体基准测试上表现强劲；在工业智能体系统中，搜索和数据分析任务上缩小了与大模型的差距。

Conclusion: AgenticQwen通过结合推理RL与智能体RL的双飞轮训练框架，有效提升了小模型在工业智能体场景下的性能，验证了方法的实用性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Modern industrial applications increasingly demand language models that act as agents, capable of multi-step reasoning and tool use in real-world settings. These tasks are typically performed under strict cost and latency constraints, making small agentic models highly desirable. In this paper, we introduce the AgenticQwen family of models, trained via multi-round reinforcement learning (RL) on synthetic data and a limited amount of open-source data. Our training framework combines reasoning RL and agentic RL with dual data flywheels that automatically generate increasingly challenging tasks. The reasoning flywheel increases task difficulty by learning from errors, while the agentic flywheel expands linear workflows into multi-branch behavior trees that better reflect the decision complexity of real-world applications. We validate AgenticQwen on public benchmarks and in an industrial agent system. The models achieve strong performance on multiple agentic benchmarks, and in our industrial agent system, close the gap with much larger models on search and data analysis tasks. Model checkpoints and part of the synthetic data: https://huggingface.co/collections/alibaba-pai/agenticqwen. Data synthesis and RL training code: https://github.com/haruhi-sudo/data_synth_and_rl. The data synthesis pipeline is also integrated into EasyDistill: https://github.com/modelscope/easydistill.

</details>


### [37] [Language as a Latent Variable for Reasoning Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21593)
*Linjuan Wu,Haoran Wei,Jialong Tang,Shuang Luo,Baosong Yang,Yongliang Shen,Weiming Lu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现非英语响应在推理任务上有时优于英语响应，提出将语言视为影响模型内部推理路径的潜在变量。基于此，设计polyGRPO框架，利用语言变化作为隐式探索信号进行强化学习，在仅用18.1K多语言数学数据训练后，在英语和多语言推理基准上分别提升6.72%和6.89%的绝对准确率，并展现出跨任务泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观察到LLM在减少英语中心化偏差后，非英语响应在推理任务上表现反超英语响应的现象，推测语言并非仅作为输出媒介，而是作为潜在变量结构性地调节模型的内部推理路径。现有方法忽视了语言多样性对模型推理空间的扩展作用。

Method: 提出Polyglot Thinking Experiment，在语言约束和语言无约束条件下对相同问题进行推理比较。基于此设计polyGRPO框架：在线生成多语言偏好数据（包含约束/无约束条件），以答案准确性和推理结构为优化目标进行策略优化。

Result: 仅用18.1K多语言数学问题（无链式思维标注），在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct基础上，英语推理测试集提升6.72%绝对准确率，多语言基准提升6.89%。唯一在仅用数学数据训练的情况下，在英语常识推理任务上超越基础LLM（提升4.9%）。

Conclusion: 将语言作为潜在变量处理能够扩展模型的潜在推理空间，带来一致且可泛化的推理性能提升，验证了多语言性对推理能力的正向调节作用。

Abstract: As LLMs reduce English-centric bias, a surprising trend emerges: non-English responses sometimes outperform English on reasoning tasks. We hypothesize that language functions as a latent variable that structurally modulates the model's internal inference pathways, rather than merely serving as an output medium. To test this, we conducted a Polyglot Thinking Experiment, in which models were prompted to solve identical problems under language-constrained and language-unconstrained conditions. Results show that non-English responses often achieve higher accuracy, and the best performance frequently occur when language is unconstrained, suggesting that multilinguality broadens the model's latent reasoning space. Based on this insight, we propose polyGRPO (Polyglot Group Relative Policy Optimization), an RL framework that treats language variation as an implicit exploration signal. It generates polyglot preference data online under language-constrained and unconstrained conditions, optimizing the policy with respect to both answer accuracy and reasoning structure. Trained on only 18.1K multilingual math problems without chain-of-thought annotations, polyGRPO improves the base model (Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct) by 6.72% absolute accuracy on four English reasoning testset and 6.89% in their multilingual benchmark. Remarkably, it is the only method that surpasses the base LLM on English commonsense reasoning task (4.9%), despite being trained solely on math data-highlighting its strong cross-task generalization. Further analysis reveals that treating language as a latent variable expands the model's latent reasoning space, yielding consistent and generalizable improvements in reasoning performance.

</details>


### [38] [Process Supervision via Verbal Critique Improves Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21611)
*Hao-Yuan Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“言语过程监督”（VPS）的无训练推理时扩展框架，通过强监督者对弱推理者进行结构化自然语言批评，在多个基准测试上显著提升性能，并将批评粒度确立为推理时扩展的新维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推理时扩展主要关注链深度、样本广度以及学习型步骤评分器（PRM）三个维度，但缺乏对**外部言语监督粒度**这一维度的系统探索。

Method: 提出VPS框架，利用强监督模型对弱推理模型的生成结果提供结构化自然语言批评，在无需梯度更新的情况下进行“生成-批评-修正”的迭代循环，直至达到预设的轮次预算R。

Result: 1) GPQA Diamond上达到94.9%（R=4），超越此前SOTA 94.1%；2) AIME 2025上弱模型得分从11.7-26.7%提升至63.3-90.0%（最高提升+63.3点）；3) 在同等计算量下，VPS比Reflexion高+8.5至+12.1点，比Self-Consistency@5高+5.0 pp（GPQA）和+8.3 pp（LiveCodeBench）；4) 性能与监督者-推理者能力差距高度正相关（Pearson r=0.90），且在错误不可言语表达时（如代码合成）性能下降。

Conclusion: 批评粒度是推理时扩展的一个有效新维度；VPS在无训练条件下显著提升弱模型推理能力；未来可结合言语与可执行方法处理混合场景。

Abstract: Inference-time scaling for LLM reasoning has focused on three axes: chain depth, sample breadth, and learned step-scorers (PRMs). We introduce a fourth axis, granularity of external verbal supervision, via Verbal Process Supervision (VPS), a training-free framework that uses structured natural-language critique from a stronger supervisor to guide an iterative generate-critique-refine loop up to a round budget R. Across GPQA Diamond, AIME 2025, and LiveCodeBench V6 (covering both closed and open models), VPS yields three key results. First, on GPQA Diamond, GPT-5.4 (High) | GPT-5.4 (Low) reaches 94.9% at R=4, surpassing the 94.1% state of the art without gradient updates. Second, on AIME 2025, VPS enables strong weak-actor rescue, boosting scores from 11.7-26.7% to 63.3-90.0% (up to +63.3 points). Third, at matched compute, VPS outperforms Reflexion by +8.5 to +12.1 points and Self-Consistency@5 by +5.0 pp (GPQA) and +8.3 pp (LiveCodeBench), isolating critique granularity as the key driver. Performance scales with the supervisor-actor capability gap (Pearson r=0.90) and degrades when errors are not linguistically expressible (e.g., code synthesis), motivating hybrid verbal-executable methods. These results establish critique granularity as a new axis of inference-time scaling.

</details>


### [39] [Multilinguality at the Edge: Developing Language Models for the Global South](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21637)
*Lester James V. Miranda,Songbo Hu,Roi Reichart,Anna Korhonen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文调查了232篇论文，探讨在非英语和硬件受限的全球南方社区中，如何结合多语言模型与边缘部署（"最后一英里"挑战），并提供了可操作建议以促进包容性语言技术发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球南方非英语社区同时面临语言多样性和基础设施限制，而多语言NLP与边缘部署研究长期分离，需要系统性理解两者结合的技术挑战与机遇。

Method: 对232篇涉及语言建模全流程（数据收集、开发、部署）的论文进行系统综述，分析多语言与边缘部署交叉领域的技术现状与挑战。

Result: 揭示了多语言模型在边缘设备部署时的技术竞争关系（如模型规模与硬件限制的冲突），并识别了该交叉领域的研究空白与开放问题。

Conclusion: 呼吁NLP生态各利益相关者关注"最后一英里"问题，通过跨领域协作推动包容、公平的语言技术发展。

Abstract: Where and how language models (LMs) are deployed determines who can benefit from them. However, there are several challenges that prevent effective deployment of LMs in non-English-speaking and hardware constrained communities in the Global South. We call this challenge the last mile: the intersection of multilinguality and edge deployment, where the goals are aligned but the technical requirements often compete. Studying these two fields together is both a need, as linguistically diverse communities often face the most severe infrastructure constraints, and an opportunity, as edge and multilingual NLP research remain largely siloed. To understand the state of the art and the challenges of combining the two areas, we survey 232 papers that tackle this problem across the language modelling pipeline, from data collection to development and deployment. We also discuss open questions and provide actionable recommendations for different stakeholders in the NLP ecosystem. Finally, we hope that this work contributes to the development of inclusive and equitable language technologies.

</details>


### [40] [Fine-Grained Perspectives: Modeling Explanations with Annotator-Specific Rationales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21667)
*Olufunke O. Sarumi,Charles Welch,Daniel Braun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个联合建模标注者标签预测与解释生成的框架，通过User Passport机制融合标注者身份与人口统计信息，并设计了后验提示解释器和前缀桥解释器两种架构。实验表明，解释建模能显著提升预测性能，前缀桥方法在标签对齐和语义一致性上更优，而后验方法词汇相似度更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注聚合标签或解聚标签，但忽略了标注者提供的解释（rationales）中蕴含的细粒度视角信号。本文旨在通过联合建模标注者特定的标签预测与解释生成，更丰富、更忠实地表征标注分歧。

Method: 基于包含解聚NLI标注和标注者解释的数据集，通过表示层面的User Passport机制将标注者身份和人口统计元数据注入模型；提出两种解释器架构：1) 后验提示解释器（post-hoc prompt-based），2) 前缀桥解释器（prefixed bridge），将标注者条件化的分类器表示直接传递到生成模型中。

Result: 引入解释建模显著提升了标注者感知分类器的预测性能；前缀桥方法在标签对齐稳定性和语义一致性上更优，后验方法在词汇相似度上更强。

Conclusion: 将解释建模为细粒度视角的表达能提供更丰富、更忠实的分歧表征，所提方法通过将标注者特合理据整合到预测和生成组件中，推进了视角主义建模的发展。

Abstract: Beyond exploring disaggregated labels for modeling perspectives, annotator rationales provide fine-grained signals of individual perspectives. In this work, we propose a framework for jointly modeling annotator-specific label prediction and corresponding explanations, fine-tuned on the annotators' provided rationales. Using a dataset with disaggregated natural language inference (NLI) annotations and annotator-provided explanations, we condition predictions on both annotator identity and demographic metadata through a representation-level User Passport mechanism. We further introduce two explainer architectures: a post-hoc prompt-based explainer and a prefixed bridge explainer that transfers annotator-conditioned classifier representations directly into a generative model. This design enables explanation generation aligned with individual annotator perspectives. Our results show that incorporating explanation modeling substantially improves predictive performance over a baseline annotator-aware classifier, with the prefixed bridge approach achieving more stable label alignment and higher semantic consistency, while the post-hoc approach yields stronger lexical similarity. These findings indicate that modeling explanations as expressions of fine-grained perspective provides a richer and more faithful representation of disagreement. The proposed approaches advance perspectivist modeling by integrating annotator-specific rationales into both predictive and generative components.

</details>


### [41] [Fixation Sequences as Time Series: A Topological Approach to Dyslexia Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21698)
*Marius Huber,David R. Reich,Lena A. Jäger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文利用持久同调（拓扑数据分析方法）对眼动追踪数据中的注视序列进行多尺度特征提取，提出新颖的过滤方法，并与传统统计特征结合构建混合模型。在哥本哈根语料库的阅读眼动数据上检测阅读障碍，混合模型优于纯传统特征方法，且提出的过滤方法优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统眼动数据分析主要依赖统计特征，未能充分捕捉注视序列中的多尺度拓扑结构信息。作者希望利用持久同调提取时间序列中的鲁棒、多尺度特征，以补充传统方法，提高阅读障碍检测性能。

Method: 将注视序列视为时间序列，设计新颖的过滤（filtration）方法进行持久同调分析，提取拓扑特征；随后将这些拓扑特征与传统统计特征融合，构建混合模型。在哥本哈根语料库（包含L1和L2阅读者的眼动扫描路径）上进行阅读障碍检测实验。

Result: 混合模型在阅读障碍检测任务上优于仅使用传统特征的方法；所提出的新颖过滤方法性能优于已有的过滤方法；拓扑特征本身能达到与已有基线方法相当的性能。

Conclusion: 持久同调能够捕获注视序列中传统统计特征无法编码的互补信息，为眼动数据分析提供了新的有效视角，尤其在阅读障碍检测任务中具有显著应用价值。

Abstract: Persistent homology, a method from topological data analysis, extracts robust, multi-scale features from data. It produces stable representations of time series by applying varying thresholds to their values (a process known as a \textit{filtration}). We develop novel filtrations for time series and introduce topological methods for the analysis of eye-tracking data, by interpreting fixation sequences as time series, and constructing ``hybrid models'' that combine topological features with traditional statistical features. We empirically evaluate our method by applying it to the task of dyslexia detection from eye-tracking-while-reading data using the Copenhagen Corpus, which contains scanpaths from dyslexic and non-dyslexic L1 and L2 readers. Our hybrid models outperform existing approaches that rely solely on traditional features, showing that persistent homology captures complementary information encoded in fixation sequences. The strength of these topological features is further underscored by their achieving performance comparable to established baseline methods. Importantly, our proposed filtrations outperform existing ones.

</details>


### [42] [AEL: Agent Evolving Learning for Open-Ended Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21725)
*Wujiang Xu,Jiaojiao Han,Minghao Guo,Kai Mei,Xi Zhu,Han Zhang,Dimitris N. Metaxas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Agent Evolving Learning (AEL)框架，通过双时间尺度机制（快速：Thompson Sampling选择记忆检索策略；慢速：LLM驱动的反思注入因果洞察）解决LLM智能体在开放环境中无法有效利用历史经验的问题。在组合投资基准上Sharpe比率达2.13±0.47，超越所有对比方法；消融实验发现"少即是多"模式，额外机制均导致性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在开放环境的多轮序贯任务中仍处于"无状态"状态，每个任务都从零开始解决，无法将过去经验转化为更好的未来行为。核心障碍不在于"记住什么"而在于"如何使用"已记住的内容，包括选择何种检索策略、如何解释先前结果以及当前策略何时需要改变。

Method: 提出AEL双时间尺度框架：(1)快速时间尺度：Thompson Sampling bandit学习在每轮选择记忆检索策略；(2)慢速时间尺度：LLM驱动的反思模块诊断失败模式，将因果洞察注入智能体的决策提示中，为其提供检索证据的解释框架。

Result: 在10个行业分散股票、208轮、5个随机种子的序贯组合基准上，AEL的Sharpe比率为2.13±0.47，优于五种已发表的自改进方法及所有非LLM基线，并在所有LLM方法中保持最低方差。九种变体消融实验显示"少即是多"模式：记忆与反思共同带来58%的累积改进，但每种额外机制（规划器进化、逐工具选择、冷启动初始化、技能提取及三种信用分配方法）均导致性能下降。

Conclusion: 智能体自改进的瓶颈在于"自我诊断如何使用"经验，而非增加架构复杂性。这挑战了"更多机制带来更好性能"的直觉，强调核心应聚焦于如何让智能体学会利用已有经验而非不断添加复杂模块。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly operate in open-ended environments spanning hundreds of sequential episodes, yet they remain largely stateless: each task is solved from scratch without converting past experience into better future behavior. The central obstacle is not \emph{what} to remember but \emph{how to use} what has been remembered, including which retrieval policy to apply, how to interpret prior outcomes, and when the current strategy itself must change. We introduce \emph{Agent Evolving Learning} (\ael{}), a two-timescale framework that addresses this obstacle. At the fast timescale, a Thompson Sampling bandit learns which memory retrieval policy to apply at each episode; at the slow timescale, LLM-driven reflection diagnoses failure patterns and injects causal insights into the agent's decision prompt, giving it an interpretive frame for the evidence it retrieves. On a sequential portfolio benchmark (10 sector-diverse tickers, 208 episodes, 5 random seeds), \ael{} achieves a Sharpe ratio of 2.13$\pm$0.47, outperforming five published self-improving methods and all non-LLM baselines while maintaining the lowest variance among all LLM-based approaches. A nine-variant ablation reveals a ``less is more'' pattern: memory and reflection together produce a 58\% cumulative improvement over the stateless baseline, yet every additional mechanism we test (planner evolution, per-tool selection, cold-start initialization, skill extraction, and three credit assignment methods) \emph{degrades} performance. This demonstrates that the bottleneck in agent self-improvement is \emph{self-diagnosing how to use} experience rather than adding architectural complexity. Code and data: https://github.com/WujiangXu/AEL.

</details>


### [43] [StructMem: Structured Memory for Long-Horizon Behavior in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21748)
*Buqiang Xu,Yijun Chen,Jizhan Fang,Ruobin Zhong,Yunzhi Yao,Yuqi Zhu,Lun Du,Shumin Deng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: StructMem是一种结构增强的分层记忆框架，通过保留事件级绑定和诱导跨事件连接，在降低token使用量、API调用和运行时的同时，提升了长程对话智能体的时间推理和多跳问答性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长程对话智能体的记忆系统面临基本权衡：扁平记忆高效但无法建模关系结构，而图记忆虽支持结构化推理但构建昂贵且脆弱。需要一种既能保留事件关系结构又高效的记忆框架。

Method: 提出StructMem框架，采用结构增强的分层记忆架构，通过时间锚定双视角（temporally anchoring dual perspectives）和周期性语义整合（periodic semantic consolidation），在保留事件级绑定的同时诱导跨事件连接。

Result: 在LoCoMo基准上，StructMem提升了时间推理和多跳问答性能，同时相比先前记忆系统显著减少了token使用量、API调用次数和运行时间。

Conclusion: StructMem通过结构化的分层记忆设计有效解决了记忆系统中的效率与结构化推理之间的权衡问题，为长程对话智能体提供了更实用的记忆方案。

Abstract: Long-term conversational agents need memory systems that capture relationships between events, not merely isolated facts, to support temporal reasoning and multi-hop question answering. Current approaches face a fundamental trade-off: flat memory is efficient but fails to model relational structure, while graph-based memory enables structured reasoning at the cost of expensive and fragile construction. To address these issues, we propose \textbf{StructMem}, a structure-enriched hierarchical memory framework that preserves event-level bindings and induces cross-event connections. By temporally anchoring dual perspectives and performing periodic semantic consolidation, StructMem improves temporal reasoning and multi-hop performance on \texttt{LoCoMo}, while substantially reducing token usage, API calls, and runtime compared to prior memory systems, see https://github.com/zjunlp/LightMem .

</details>

Abstract: LLMs have been showing limitations when it comes to cultural coverage and competence, and in some cases show regional biases such as amplifying Western and Anglocentric viewpoints. While there have been works analysing the cultural capabilities of LLMs, there has not been specific work on highlighting LLM regional preferences when it comes to cultural-related questions. In this work, we propose a new dataset based on a comprehensive taxonomy of Culture-Related Open Questions (CROQ). The results show that, contrary to previous cultural bias work, LLMs show a clear tendency towards countries such as Japan. Moveover, our results show that when prompting in languages such as English or other high-resource ones, LLMs tend to provide more diverse outputs and show less inclinations towards answering questions highlighting countries for which the input language is an official language. Finally, we also investigate at which point of LLM training this cultural bias emerges, with our results suggesting that the first clear signs appear after supervised fine-tuning, and not during pre-training.

*Hans Ole Hatzel,Ekaterina Artemova,Haimo Paul Stiemer,Evelyn Gius,Chris Biemann*

TL;DR: 本文介绍了NSNRL共享任务，通过三元组二元分类（判断哪个故事与锚点故事更相似）来操作化叙事相似性，构建了包含1000+故事摘要三元组的标注数据集，并评估了71个提交系统。LLM集成在分类任务中表现最佳，而嵌入任务中预/后处理与微调方案性能相当，两个赛道均存在改进空间。


<details>

Result: 收到46个团队的71个最终提交。分类赛道中，LLM集成系统占据多数顶级排名；嵌入赛道中，对预训练嵌入模型进行预/后处理的系统与定制微调方案性能相当。两个赛道均存在改进空间。任务网站提供嵌入可视化及所有团队的实例级分类结果。

Conclusion: 叙事相似性可通过三元组分类任务有效操作化，LLM集成在分类中表现突出，但现有系统仍有提升空间；嵌入表征方面，轻量级预/后处理可接近微调效果，表明预训练模型已蕴含一定叙事结构信息。

Abstract: We present the shared task on narrative similarity and narrative representation learning - NSNRL (pronounced "nass-na-rel"). The task operationalizes narrative similarity as a binary classification problem: determining which of two stories is more similar to an anchor story. We introduce a novel definition of narrative similarity, compatible with both narrative theory and intuitive judgment. Based on the similarity judgments collected under this concept, we also evaluate narrative embedding representations. We collected at least two annotations each for more than 1,000 story summary triples, with each annotation being backed by at least two annotators in agreement. This paper describes the sampling and annotation process for the dataset; further, we give an overview of the submitted systems and the techniques they employ. We received a total of 71 final submissions from 46 teams across our two tracks. In our triple-based classification setup, LLM ensembles make up many of the top-scoring systems, while in the embedding setup, systems with pre- and post-processing on pretrained embedding models perform about on par with custom fine-tuned solutions. Our analysis identifies potential headroom for improvement of automated systems in both tracks. The task website includes visualizations of embeddings alongside instance-level classification results for all teams.

</details>


### [46] [Machine Behavior in Relational Moral Dilemmas: Moral Rightness, Predicted Human Behavior, and Model Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21871)
*Jiseon Kim,Jea Kwon,Luiz Felipe Vecchietti,Wenchao Dong,Jaehong Kim,Meeyoung Cha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型在道德判断中如何处理情境依赖性和人际关系因素。通过"举报者困境"实验，对比三种视角（道德正确性、预测的人类行为、自主决策），发现LLM的决策虽在道德正确性上保持公平导向，但缺乏其内部世界模型中已编码的社会敏感性（如人际关系的影响），导致决策与预测行为不一致，存在实际部署中的对齐风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM被用作决策支持系统，确定它们是否编码了人类道德判断中依赖于情境和人际关系的细微社会差异至关重要。

Method: 使用"举报者困境"范式，操纵两个实验维度（犯罪严重程度和人际关系亲近度），评估三种视角：（1）道德正确性（规范性规范）、（2）预测的人类行为（描述性社会期望）、（3）自主模型决策。

Result: 发现清晰的跨视角分歧：道德正确性保持公平导向，而预测的人类行为随关系亲近度显著转向忠诚；模型决策与道德正确性判断对齐而非其自身的行为预测。

Conclusion: LLM决策优先考虑僵化的规范性规则，而非其内部世界建模中已存在的社会敏感性，这种不一致性可能导致实际部署中的显著错位。

Abstract: Human moral judgment is context-dependent and modulated by interpersonal relationships. As large language models (LLMs) increasingly function as decision-support systems, determining whether they encode these social nuances is critical. We characterize machine behavior using the Whistleblower's Dilemma by varying two experimental dimensions: crime severity and relational closeness. Our study evaluates three distinct perspectives: (1) moral rightness (prescriptive norms), (2) predicted human behavior (descriptive social expectations), and (3) autonomous model decision-making. By analyzing the reasoning processes, we identify a clear cross-perspective divergence: while moral rightness remains consistently fairness-oriented, predicted human behavior shifts significantly toward loyalty as relational closeness increases. Crucially, model decisions align with moral rightness judgments rather than their own behavioral predictions. This inconsistency suggests that LLM decision-making prioritizes rigid, prescriptive rules over the social sensitivity present in their internal world-modeling, which poses a gap that may lead to significant misalignments in real-world deployments.

</details>


### [47] [Revisiting Non-Verbatim Memorization in Large Language Models: The Role of Entity Surface Forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21882)
*Yuto Nishida,Naoki Shikoda,Yosuke Kishinami,Ryo Fujii,Makoto Morishita,Hidetaka Kamigaito,Taro Watanabe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文引入RedirectQA数据集，利用维基百科重定向信息将实体事实三元组与多种表面形式（别名、缩写、拼写变体、常见错误形式）关联。通过对13个LLM的实验发现，模型的事实记忆结果会随实体表面形式变化而改变，这种不一致性具有类别依赖性：对拼写变体更鲁棒，对别名和缩写更敏感。事实记忆既非纯粹表面特异，也非完全表面不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于实体的事实记忆评估通常使用单一规范表面形式查询每个实体，难以区分"记忆事实本身"与"通过特定名称访问事实"，需要更系统的评估框架来理解LLM的非逐字记忆行为。

Method: 构建RedirectQA数据集，利用维基百科重定向信息将Wikidata事实三元组与实体的多种分类表面形式（别名、缩写、拼写变体、常见错误形式）关联；在13个LLM上评估表面条件化的事实记忆，分析不同表面形式对预测结果的影响。

Result: 1) 实体表面形式变化会导致预测结果改变，这种不一致性具有类别依赖性；2) 模型对拼写变体等微小正字法变化更鲁棒，对别名和缩写等更大词汇变化更敏感；3) 实体级别和表面级别频率都与准确性相关，实体频率的贡献往往超出表面频率。

Conclusion: LLM的事实记忆既非纯粹表面特异（完全依赖特定名称）也非完全表面不变（与名称无关），表面形式的多样性在评估非逐字记忆时至关重要。

Abstract: Understanding what kinds of factual knowledge large language models (LLMs) memorize is essential for evaluating their reliability and limitations. Entity-based QA is a common framework for analyzing non-verbatim memorization, but typical evaluations query each entity using a single canonical surface form, making it difficult to disentangle fact memorization from access through a particular name. We introduce RedirectQA, an entity-based QA dataset that uses Wikipedia redirect information to associate Wikidata factual triples with categorized surface forms for each entity, including alternative names, abbreviations, spelling variants, and common erroneous forms. Across 13 LLMs, we examine surface-conditioned factual memorization and find that prediction outcomes often change when only the entity surface form changes. This inconsistency is category-dependent: models are more robust to minor orthographic variations than to larger lexical variations such as aliases and abbreviations. Frequency analyses further suggest that both entity- and surface-level frequencies are associated with accuracy, and that entity frequency often contributes beyond surface frequency. Overall, factual memorization appears neither purely surface-specific nor fully surface-invariant, highlighting the importance of surface-form diversity in evaluating non-verbatim memorization.

</details>


### [48] [A Multimodal Text- and Graph-Based Approach for Open-Domain Event Extraction from Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21885)
*Praval Sharma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为MODEE的多模态开放域事件抽取方法，通过结合图学习与LLM文本表示来建模文档级推理，在开放域和封闭域事件抽取任务上均超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件抽取方法存在两个主要局限：(1)封闭域算法受限于预定义事件类型，难以泛化到未见类型；(2)开放域算法未能充分利用大语言模型(LLM)的先进能力，且缺乏对文档级上下文、结构和语义推理的显式建模，而LLM在长文本中面临"中间丢失"和注意力稀释等问题。

Method: 提出MODEE方法，将基于图的文档结构学习与LLM的文本表示相结合，实现对文档级推理的显式建模，从而完成开放域事件抽取。

Result: 在大数据集上的实证评估表明，MODEE在开放域事件抽取中优于现有最先进方法，并且可泛化到封闭域事件抽取任务，同样超越已有算法。

Conclusion: 图学习与LLM文本表示的融合能有效解决开放域事件抽取中缺乏文档级推理的问题，且该方法具有良好的跨域泛化能力。

Abstract: Event extraction is essential for event understanding and analysis. It supports tasks such as document summarization and decision-making in emergency scenarios. However, existing event extraction approaches have limitations: (1) closed-domain algorithms are restricted to predefined event types and thus rarely generalize to unseen types and (2) open-domain event extraction algorithms, capable of handling unconstrained event types, have largely overlooked the potential of large language models (LLMs) despite their advanced abilities. Additionally, they do not explicitly model document-level contextual, structural, and semantic reasoning, which are crucial for effective event extraction but remain challenging for LLMs due to lost-in-the-middle phenomenon and attention dilution. To address these limitations, we propose multimodal open-domain event extraction, MODEE , a novel approach for open-domain event extraction that combines graph-based learning with text-based representation from LLMs to model document-level reasoning. Empirical evaluations on large datasets demonstrate that MODEE outperforms state-of-the-art open-domain event extraction approaches and can be generalized to closed-domain event extraction, where it outperforms existing algorithms.

</details>


### [49] [TingIS: Real-time Risk Event Discovery from Noisy Customer Incidents at Enterprise Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21889)
*Jun Wang,Ziyin Zhang,Rui Wang,Hang Yu,Peng Di,Rui Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TingIS是一个端到端的企业级事件发现系统，通过多阶段事件链接引擎（结合高效索引与LLM）、级联路由机制和多维降噪流水线，在生产环境（日处理30万条消息）中实现P90告警延迟3.5分钟、高优先级事件95%发现率，显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模云原生服务中，技术异常实时检测与缓解至关重要，但客户事件数据存在极端噪声、高吞吐量和语义复杂性，从这些数据中提取可操作情报极具挑战。

Method: 提出TingIS端到端系统，核心为多阶段事件链接引擎（协同高效索引技术与LLM进行事件合并决策），辅以级联路由机制（精确业务归属）和多维降噪流水线（集成领域知识、统计模式和行为过滤）。

Result: 在生产环境（峰值每分钟2000+消息，每日30万消息）中，P90告警延迟3.5分钟，高优先级事件发现率95%；在路由准确率、聚类质量和信噪比上显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: TingIS有效解决了云原生环境下从高噪声客户事件数据中提取可操作情报的难题，展示了结合LLM与传统索引技术在企业级事件发现中的实用价值。

Abstract: Real-time detection and mitigation of technical anomalies are critical for large-scale cloud-native services, where even minutes of downtime can result in massive financial losses and diminished user trust. While customer incidents serve as a vital signal for discovering risks missed by monitoring, extracting actionable intelligence from this data remains challenging due to extreme noise, high throughput, and semantic complexity of diverse business lines. In this paper, we present TingIS, an end-to-end system designed for enterprise-grade incident discovery. At the core of TingIS is a multi-stage event linking engine that synergizes efficient indexing techniques with Large Language Models (LLMs) to make informed decisions on event merging, enabling the stable extraction of actionable incidents from just a handful of diverse user descriptions. This engine is complemented by a cascaded routing mechanism for precise business attribution and a multi-dimensional noise reduction pipeline that integrates domain knowledge, statistical patterns, and behavioral filtering. Deployed in a production environment handling a peak throughput of over 2,000 messages per minute and 300,000 messages per day, TingIS achieves a P90 alert latency of 3.5 minutes and a 95\% discovery rate for high-priority incidents. Benchmarks constructed from real-world data demonstrate that TingIS significantly outperforms baseline methods in routing accuracy, clustering quality, and Signal-to-Noise Ratio.

</details>


### [50] [EVENT5Ws: A Large Dataset for Open-Domain Event Extraction from Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21890)
*Praval Sharma,Ashok Samal,Leen-Kiat Soh,Deepti Joshi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文创建了EVENT5Ws，一个大规模、人工标注且统计验证的开放域事件抽取数据集，并基于该数据集评估了当前最先进的预训练大语言模型，建立了基准，验证了模型在不同地理上下文中的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件抽取数据集存在局限：封闭域下事件类型覆盖有限，开放域下缺乏大规模人工验证的数据集。为促进自动化事件抽取方法的发展，需要构建更高质量的数据集。

Method: 设计系统化的标注流程创建EVENT5Ws数据集，包含人工标注和统计验证；基于该数据集评估预训练大语言模型，并测试模型在不同地理上下文数据集上的泛化能力。

Result: 建立了开放域事件抽取的基准结果；在EVENT5Ws上训练的模型能够有效泛化到不同地理上下文的数据集。

Conclusion: EVENT5Ws数据集能够支持开放域事件抽取算法开发，具备良好的泛化性；总结了大规模数据集开发的经验教训和建议。

Abstract: Event extraction identifies the central aspects of events from text. It supports event understanding and analysis, which is crucial for tasks such as informed decision-making in emergencies. Therefore, it is necessary to develop automated event extraction approaches. However, existing datasets for algorithm development have limitations, including limited coverage of event types in closed-domain settings and a lack of large, manually verified dataset in open-domain settings. To address these limitations, we create EVENT5Ws , a large, manually annotated, and statistically verified open-domain event extraction dataset. We design a systematic annotation pipeline to create the dataset and provide empirical insights into annotation complexity. Using EVENT5Ws, we evaluate state-of-the-art pre-trained large language models and establish a benchmark for future research. We further show that models trained on EVENT5Ws generalize effectively to datasets from different geographical contexts, which demonstrates its potential for developing generalizable algorithms. Finally, we summarize the lessons learned during the dataset development and provide recommendations to support future large-scale dataset development.

</details>


### [51] [Mapping the Political Discourse in the Brazilian Chamber of Deputies: A Multi-Faceted Computational Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21897)
*Flávio Soriano,Victoria F. Mello,Pedro B. Rigueira,Gisele L. Pappa,Wagner Meira,Ana Paula Couto da Silva,Jussara M. Almeida*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个结合历时文体分析、上下文主题建模和语义聚类的计算框架，用于分析议会话语的多维特征（如何说、说什么、谁在说）。通过对巴西众议院2003-2025年超过45万篇演讲的分析，发现长期的文体转向更短更直接的演讲、国家危机下立法议程的剧烈转向，以及区域和性别身份在话语联盟中比政党 affiliation 更显著的规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的立法行为分析过度依赖投票记录，忽略了政治演讲中丰富的语义和修辞内容，缺乏对议会话语多维度特征的系统性分析方法。

Method: 构建了一个可扩展且可泛化的计算框架，融合了历时文体分析（diachronic stylometric analysis）、上下文主题建模（contextual topic modeling）和议员演讲的语义聚类（semantic clustering），从"如何说、说什么、谁在说"三个互补维度分析议会话语。

Result: 发现了长期文体风格向更短更直接演讲的转变；国家危机下立法议程的剧烈重新定向；以及区域和性别身份在话语相似性上往往比正式政党 affiliation 更显著的现象。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了分析议会话语作为多维现象的稳健方法论，补充了传统的基于投票的立法行为分析方法。

Abstract: Analyses of legislative behavior often rely on voting records, overlooking the rich semantic and rhetorical content of political speech. In this paper, we ask three complementary questions about parliamentary discourse: how things are said, what is being said, and who is speaking in discursively similar ways. To answer these questions, we introduce a scalable and generalizable computational framework that combines diachronic stylometric analysis, contextual topic modeling, and semantic clustering of deputies' speeches. We apply this framework to a large-scale case study of the Brazilian Chamber of Deputies, using a corpus of over 450,000 speeches from 2003 to 2025. Our results show a long-term stylistic shift toward shorter and more direct speeches, a legislative agenda that reorients sharply in response to national crises, and a granular map of discursive alignments in which regional and gender identities often prove more salient than formal party affiliation. More broadly, this work offers a robust methodology for analyzing parliamentary discourse as a multidimensional phenomenon that complements traditional vote-based approaches.

</details>


### [52] [GiVA: Gradient-Informed Bases for Vector-Based Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21901)
*Neeraj Gangwar,Rishabh Deshmukh,Michael Shavlovsky,Hancao Li,Vivek Mittal,Lexing Ying,Nickvash Kani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出GiVA，一种基于梯度的初始化策略用于向量适配方法，在保持极端参数效率的同时，训练时间与LoRA相当，并将秩需求降低8倍，在NLU、NLG和图像分类任务上超越或达到与现有方法竞争的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向量适配方法虽然参数效率极高，但需要比LoRA高得多的秩才能匹配其性能，导致训练成本增加。

Method: 提出GiVA（Gradient-based Initialization for Vector-based Adaptation），一种基于梯度的初始化策略，用于向量适配方法。

Result: 在自然语言理解、自然语言生成和图像分类等多个基准上，GiVA一致优于或达到与现有向量适配方法和LoRA竞争的性能，同时将秩需求降低8倍。

Conclusion: GiVA成功实现了训练时间与LoRA相当、保持向量适配极端参数效率的优势，并显著降低了秩需求。

Abstract: As model sizes continue to grow, parameter-efficient fine-tuning has emerged as a powerful alternative to full fine-tuning. While LoRA is widely adopted among these methods, recent research has explored vector-based adaptation methods due to their extreme parameter efficiency. However, these methods typically require substantially higher ranks than LoRA to match its performance, leading to increased training costs. This work introduces GiVA, a gradient-based initialization strategy for vector-based adaptation. It achieves training times comparable to LoRA and maintains the extreme parameter efficiency of vector-based adaptation. We evaluate GiVA across diverse benchmarks, including natural language understanding, natural language generation, and image classification. Experiments show that our approach consistently outperforms or achieves performance competitive with existing vector-based adaptation methods and LoRA while reducing rank requirements by a factor of eight ($8\times$).

</details>


### [53] [Mango: Multi-Agent Web Navigation via Global-View Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18779)
*Weixi Tong,Yifeng Di,Tianyi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Mango是一种多智能体网页导航方法，通过将URL选择建模为多臂老虎机问题并采用汤普森采样动态分配导航预算，结合情节记忆组件存储导航历史，在WebVoyager和WebWalkerQA上分别超越最佳基线7.3%和26.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网页智能体通常从根URL开始探索，对于深层层次结构的复杂网站效率低下，缺乏网站全局视图导致频繁陷入导航陷阱、探索无关分支或在有限预算内无法到达目标信息。

Method: 提出Mango多智能体网页导航方法：1) 利用网站结构动态确定最优起始点；2) 将URL选择建模为多臂老虎机问题，采用汤普森采样自适应分配导航预算；3) 引入情节记忆组件存储导航历史，使智能体从先前尝试中学习。

Result: 在WebVoyager上使用GPT-5-mini达到63.6%成功率，超越最佳基线7.3%；在WebWalkerQA上达到52.5%成功率，超越最佳基线26.8%。在开源和闭源模型上均展现出泛化能力。

Conclusion: Mango通过结构化起始点选择、自适应预算分配和记忆机制显著提升了网页导航效率和成功率，数据和代码已开源。

Abstract: Existing web agents typically initiate exploration from the root URL, which is inefficient for complex websites with deep hierarchical structures. Without a global view of the website's structure, agents frequently fall into navigation traps, explore irrelevant branches, or fail to reach target information within a limited budget. We propose Mango, a multi-agent web navigation method that leverages the website structure to dynamically determine optimal starting points. We formulate URL selection as a multi-armed bandit problem and employ Thompson Sampling to adaptively allocate the navigation budget across candidate URLs. Furthermore, we introduce an episodic memory component to store navigation history, enabling the agent to learn from previous attempts. Experiments on WebVoyager demonstrate that Mango achieves a success rate of 63.6% when using GPT-5-mini, outperforming the best baseline by 7.3%. Furthermore, on WebWalkerQA, Mango attains a 52.5% success rate, surpassing the best baseline by 26.8%. We also demonstrate the generalizability of Mango using both open-source and closed-source models as backbones. Our data and code are open-source and available at https://github.com/VichyTong/Mango.

</details>


### [54] [MathDuels: Evaluating LLMs as Problem Posers and Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21916)
*Zhiqiu Xu,Shibo Jin,Shreya Arya,Mayur Naik*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MathDuels 是一个自对弈基准测试，让模型同时扮演问题出题者和解题者，通过对抗性提示生成问题，并使用 Rasch 模型联合评估出题能力和解题能力。实验发现两种能力部分解耦，且该基准可随模型能力进步而动态演化，避免静态基准的天花板效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数学基准在静态问题集上已达天花板效应，无法有效区分前沿模型的能力差异。

Method: 构建 MathDuels 自对弈基准：模型同时担任出题者（通过元提示、问题生成、难度放大三阶段流水线生成问题）和解题者。使用独立验证器过滤病题，采用 Rasch 模型联合估计解题能力和问题难度，出题质量由所出问题的难度导出。

Result: 在 19 个前沿模型上的实验表明，出题能力与解题能力部分解耦；双角色评估能够揭示单一角色基准中无法显现的能力差异；新模型能够产生击败先前主导模型的问题，使基准难度随参与者能力共演。

Conclusion: MathDuels 通过引入自对弈出题-解题双角色评估，突破了静态基准的饱和限制，实现了基准难度与模型能力的协同进化，为前沿语言模型的数学能力评估提供了更具区分度的新范式。

Abstract: As frontier language models attain near-ceiling performance on static mathematical benchmarks, existing evaluations are increasingly unable to differentiate model capabilities, largely because they cast models solely as solvers of fixed problem sets. We introduce MathDuels, a self-play benchmark in which models occupy dual roles: each authors math problems under adversarial prompting and solves problems authored by every other participant. Problems are produced through a three-stage generation pipeline (meta-prompting, problem generation, and difficulty amplification), and validated by an independent verifier that excludes ill-posed questions. A Rasch model (Rasch, 1993) jointly estimates solver abilities and problem difficulties; author quality is derived from the difficulties of each model's authored problems. Experiments across 19 frontier models reveal that authoring and solving capabilities are partially decoupled, and that dual-role evaluation reveals capability separations invisible in single-role benchmarks. As newer models enter the arena, they produce problems that defeat previously dominant solvers, so the benchmark's difficulty co-evolves with participant strength rather than saturating at a fixed ceiling. We host a public leaderboard that updates as new models are released.

</details>


### [55] [Evaluation of Automatic Speech Recognition Using Generative Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21928)
*Thibault Bañeras-Roux,Shashi Kumar,Driss Khalil,Sergio Burdisso,Petr Motlicek,Shiran Liu,Mickael Rouvier,Jane Wottawa,Richard Dufour*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探索了基于解码器的大语言模型（LLMs）在语义语音识别评估中的应用，在HATS数据集上，最佳LLM在假设选择任务中与人类标注者的一致性达92-94%，远超WER（63%）及其他语义指标，并展示了LLM在可解释语义评估中的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统WER指标对语义不敏感，而基于嵌入的语义指标虽与人类感知相关性更好，但基于解码器的LLMs在该任务中尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 通过三种方式评估LLM相关性：(1)从两个候选假设中选择最佳假设；(2)使用生成式嵌入计算语义距离；(3)对错误进行定性分类。

Result: 在HATS数据集上，最佳LLM在假设选择任务中与人类标注者一致性达92-94%，远高于WER的63%，且优于其他语义指标。基于解码器的LLM嵌入性能与编码器模型相当。

Conclusion: LLM为可解释且语义感知的ASR评估提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) is traditionally evaluated using Word Error Rate (WER), a metric that is insensitive to meaning. Embedding-based semantic metrics are better correlated with human perception, but decoder-based Large Language Models (LLMs) remain underexplored for this task. This paper evaluates their relevance through three approaches: (1) selecting the best hypothesis between two candidates, (2) computing semantic distance using generative embeddings, and (3) qualitative classification of errors. On the HATS dataset, the best LLMs achieve 92--94\% agreement with human annotators for hypothesis selection, compared to 63\% for WER, also outperforming semantic metrics. Embeddings from decoder-based LLMs show performance comparable to encoder models. Finally, LLMs offer a promising direction for interpretable and semantic ASR evaluation.

</details>

### [56] [Sensitivity Uncertainty Alignment in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20903)
*Prakul Sunil Hiremath,Harshit R. Hiremath*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出敏感性-不确定性对齐(SUA)框架，通过校准预测稳定性与模型不确定性来检测大语言模型在对抗性和模糊输入下的失败模式，并引入SUA-TR训练方法与放弃规则以提升模型可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在对抗性输入和模糊输入下常表现出预测不稳定但过度自信的问题，现有方法（如熵或自一致性）难以有效识别此类失败。作者认为对抗敏感性和模糊性反映了一个共同问题：预测不稳定与模型不确定性之间的错位。

Method: 定义标量分数 SUA_θ(x) 捕捉分布敏感性与预测熵之间的差异；证明最小化其正部可约束最坏情况下的扰动风险并与校准误差相关；形式化"模糊坍塌"现象；提出SUA-TR训练方法，结合一致性正则化与熵对齐，并设计放弃规则以实现更安全的推理。

Result: 在问答和分类等任务上，SUA框架相比单独使用熵或自一致性方法，能更准确地识别模型失败。

Conclusion: SUA是模型无关的框架，为提升不断演化的大语言模型的可靠性提供了基础。

Abstract: We propose Sensitivity-Uncertainty Alignment (SUA), a framework for analyzing failures of large language models under adversarial and ambiguous inputs. We argue that adversarial sensitivity and ambiguity reflect a common issue: misalignment between prediction instability and model uncertainty. A reliable model should express higher uncertainty when its predictions are unstable; failure to do so leads to miscalibration.
  We define a scalar score, SUA_theta(x), capturing the difference between distributional sensitivity and predictive entropy. We show that minimizing its positive part bounds worst-case perturbed risk and relates to calibration error. We also formalize ambiguity collapse, where models produce overconfident outputs despite multiple valid interpretations.
  We introduce SUA-TR, a training method combining consistency regularization and entropy alignment, along with an abstention rule for safer inference. Across tasks including question answering and classification, SUA better identifies model failures than entropy or self-consistency alone.
  The framework is model-agnostic and provides a basis for improving reliability in evolving language models.

</details>


### [57] [Hidden Secrets in the arXiv: Discovering, Analyzing, and Preventing Unintentional Information Disclosure in Source Files of Scientific Preprints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20927)
*Jan Pennekamp,Johannes Lohmöller,David Schütte,Joscha Loos,Martin Henze*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了arXiv上270万份提交的源文件，发现几乎所有提交都包含某种形式的"隐藏"信息（如不必要的文件、嵌入元数据、源码注释等），且现有清理工具效果不佳，作者因此提出了ALC-NG工具用于全面清理非编译所需的文件、元数据和注释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: arXiv公开了论文的LaTeX源码等文件，这可能导致敏感信息意外泄露（如API密钥、Git历史等），但目前缺乏对该问题的大规模系统性研究。

Method: 对arXiv上270万份有源文件的提交，从三个维度分析信息泄露：不必要的文件、文件中嵌入的元数据、文件中的无关内容（如源码注释）。

Result: 几乎所有arXiv提交都包含某种形式的隐藏信息，包括可编辑文档链接、API密钥、私钥、完整的Git历史等；现有清理工具无法可靠地清除这些信息。

Conclusion: 为缓解信息泄露问题，作者提供了ALC-NG工具，能够全面移除编译LaTeX论文所不需要的文件、元数据和注释。

Abstract: Preprints are essential for the timely and open dissemination of research. arXiv, the most widely used preprint service, takes the idea of open science one step further by not only publishing the actual preprints but also LaTeX sources and other files used to create them. As known from other contexts, such as GitHub repositories, and anecdotally exemplified for arXiv, making source code publicly available risks disclosing otherwise "hidden" information. Consequently, the public availability of paper sources raises the question of how much sensitive content is (unintentionally) disclosed through them.
  In this paper, we systematically answer this question for all 2.7M arXiv submissions with available source files across three dimensions of source file-induced information disclosure: (1) inclusion of unnecessary files, (2) metadata embedded in files, and (3) irrelevant content in files such as source code comments. Our analysis reveals that nearly every arXiv submission contains some form of "hidden" information. Notable findings range from links to editable web documents for internal coordination over API and private keys to complete Git histories.
  While different tools promise to remove such information from source files, we show that they fail to reliably achieve the intended cleaning functionality. To mitigate this situation, we provide ALC-NG to comprehensively remove files, metadata, and comments that are not needed to compile a LaTeX paper.

</details>


### [58] [SafeRedirect: Defeating Internal Safety Collapse via Task-Completion Redirection in Frontier LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20930)
*Chao Pan,Yu Wu,Xin Yao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SafeRedirect是一种系统级防御方法，通过重定向模型的任务完成驱动力而非压制它，有效破解了前沿LLM在执行合法专业任务时自发产生有害内容的内部安全崩溃（ISC）问题，将平均不安全内容生成率从71.2%降至8.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿LLM存在一种称为内部安全崩溃（ISC）的失效模式，即当模型执行结构上需要有害内容才能正确完成的合法专业任务时，会自发产生有害内容，安全失败率超过95%。现有输入级防御对此完全无效（100%失败率），标准系统提示防御也仅提供部分缓解。

Method: 提出SafeRedirect，一种系统级覆盖方法。核心思路是重定向模型的任务完成驱动力而非压制它：授予明确的任务失败许可、规定确定性硬停止输出、指示模型保留有害占位符不解析。在7个前沿LLM上、3种AI/ML相关ISC任务类型的单轮设置下进行评估。

Result: SafeRedirect将平均不安全内容生成率从71.2%降至8.0%，而最强可行基线为55.0%。多模型消融实验表明失败许可和条件特异性是关键要素。跨攻击评估证实SafeRedirect对ISC具有最先进的防御能力，在其他攻击家族上的泛化性能至少与基线持平。

Conclusion: SafeRedirect通过任务重定向而非抑制的方式有效解决了ISC问题，在多个前沿模型上显著降低了不安全生成率，且具有良好的泛化性能。

Abstract: Internal Safety Collapse (ISC) is a failure mode in which frontier LLMs, when executing legitimate professional tasks whose correct completion structurally requires harmful content, spontaneously generate that content with safety failure rates exceeding 95%. Existing input-level defenses achieve a 100% failure rate against ISC, and standard system prompt defenses provide only partial mitigation. We propose SafeRedirect, a system-level override that defeats ISC by redirecting the model's task-completion drive rather than suppressing it. SafeRedirect grants explicit permission to fail the task, prescribes a deterministic hard-stop output, and instructs the model to preserve harmful placeholders unresolved. Evaluated on seven frontier LLMs across three AI/ML-related ISC task types in the single-turn setting, SafeRedirect reduces average unsafe generation rates from 71.2% to 8.0%, compared to 55.0% for the strongest viable baseline. Multi-model ablation reveals that failure permission and condition specificity are universally critical, while the importance of other components varies across models. Cross-attack evaluation confirms state-of-the-art defense against ISC with generalization performance at least on par with the baseline on other attack families. Code is available at https://github.com/fzjcdt/SafeRedirect.

</details>


### [59] [Adaptive Defense Orchestration for RAG: A Sentinel-Strategist Architecture against Multi-Vector Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20932)
*Pranav Pallerla,Wilson Naik Bhukya,Bharath Vemula,Charan Ramtej Kodi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Sentinel-Strategist架构，通过上下文感知的异常检测和选择性防御部署，在RAG系统中显著降低了安全性与检索效用之间的权衡，将防御带来的上下文召回率损失从40%+恢复至接近无防御基线水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统在医疗、法律等敏感领域依赖私有领域知识，面临成员推断、数据投毒和内容泄露等严重安全风险。全面启用所有防御虽可缓解风险，但会带来巨大的效用成本（实验显示上下文召回率下降超40%），因此亟需一种能动态平衡安全性与效用的方法。

Method: 提出Sentinel-Strategist架构：Sentinel组件负责检测异常的检索行为，Strategist组件根据查询上下文选择性部署仅必要的防御措施。在三个基准数据集和五个编排模型上评估了自适应防御编排（ADO）方法。

Result: ADO方法完全消除了MBA风格的成员推断泄露，同时大幅恢复了检索效用，接近未防御基线水平。在数据投毒攻击下，最强的ADO变体将攻击成功率降至接近零，并将上下文召回率恢复至未防御基线的75%以上，但鲁棒性对模型选择敏感。

Conclusion: 自适应的查询感知防御能够显著降低RAG系统中的安全-效用权衡，为敏感领域RAG系统的实际部署提供了可行的安全方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems are increasingly deployed in sensitive domains such as healthcare and law, where they rely on private, domain-specific knowledge. This capability introduces significant security risks, including membership inference, data poisoning, and unintended content leakage. A straightforward mitigation is to enable all relevant defenses simultaneously, but doing so incurs a substantial utility cost. In our experiments, an always-on defense stack reduces contextual recall by more than 40%, indicating that retrieval degradation is the primary failure mode. To mitigate this trade-off in RAG systems, we propose the Sentinel-Strategist architecture, a context-aware framework for risk analysis and defense selection. A Sentinel detects anomalous retrieval behavior, after which a Strategist selectively deploys only the defenses warranted by the query context. Evaluated across three benchmark datasets and five orchestration models, ADO is shown to eliminate MBA-style membership inference leakage while substantially recovering retrieval utility relative to a fully static defense stack, approaching undefended baseline levels. Under data poisoning, the strongest ADO variants reduce attack success to near zero while restoring contextual recall to more than 75% of the undefended baseline, although robustness remains sensitive to model choice. Overall, these findings show that adaptive, query-aware defense can substantially reduce the security-utility trade-off in RAG systems.

</details>


### [60] [Breaking Bad: Interpretability-Based Safety Audits of State-of-the-Art LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20945)
*Krishiv Agarwal,Ramneet Kaur,Colin Samplawski,Manoj Acharya,Anirban Roy,Daniel Elenius,Brian Matejek,Adam D. Cobb,Susmit Jha*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于可解释性的红队攻击方法，对8个开源大语言模型进行安全审计，通过自适应两阶段网格搜索算法优化激活导向系数，发现Llama-3系列极易被越狱（最高91%成功率），而GPT-oss-120B最为鲁棒，揭示了可解释性导向在安全审计中的强大能力及其双重用途风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型安全审计多依赖黑盒探测方法，难以系统性地揭示模型内部机制中的脆弱性，亟需基于模型内部表征的审计工具来更有效地发现安全漏洞。

Method: 采用通用导向（Universal Steering, US）和表征工程（Representation Engineering, RepE）两种可解释性方法，引入自适应两阶段网格搜索算法来寻找不安全行为概念的最优激活导向系数，并在8个开源LLM上进行评估。

Result: Llama-3.3-70B-4bt在US和RepE方法下分别有高达91%和83%的越狱响应率；GPT-oss-120B对两种可解释性攻击均表现出鲁棒性；Qwen和Phi系列中，小模型越狱率较低，而大模型更易被攻击。

Conclusion: 可解释性导向是一种强大的系统性安全审计工具，但同时也存在双重用途风险，LLM在部署时需要更好的内部防御机制。

Abstract: Effective safety auditing of large language models (LLMs) demands tools that go beyond black-box probing and systematically uncover vulnerabilities rooted in model internals. We present a comprehensive, interpretability-driven jailbreaking audit of eight SOTA open-source LLMs: Llama-3.1-8B, Llama-3.3-70B-4bt, GPT-oss- 20B, GPT-oss-120B, Qwen3-0.6B, Qwen3-32B, Phi4-3.8B, and Phi4-14B. Leveraging interpretability-based approaches -- Universal Steering (US) and Representation Engineering (RepE) -- we introduce an adaptive two-stage grid search algorithm to identify optimal activation-steering coefficients for unsafe behavioral concepts. Our evaluation, conducted on a curated set of harmful queries and a standardized LLM-based judging protocol, reveals stark contrasts in model robustness. The Llama-3 models are highly vulnerable, with up to 91\% (US) and 83\% (RepE) jailbroken responses on Llama-3.3-70B-4bt, while GPT-oss-120B remains robust to attacks via both interpretability approaches. Qwen and Phi models show mixed results, with the smaller Qwen3-0.6B and Phi4-3.8B mostly exhibiting lower jailbreaking rates, while their larger counterparts are more susceptible. Our results establish interpretability-based steering as a powerful tool for systematic safety audits, but also highlight its dual-use risks and the need for better internal defenses in LLM deployment.

</details>


### [61] [Breaking MCP with Function Hijacking Attacks: Novel Threats for Function Calling and Agentic Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20994)
*Yannis Belkhiter,Giulio Zizzo,Sergio Maffeis,Seshu Tirupathi,John D. Kelleher*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对智能体模型工具选择过程的函数劫持攻击（FHA），能够强制模型调用攻击者指定的函数。该攻击对上下文语义不敏感、对函数集鲁棒，且可训练生成通用对抗函数。在5种模型上（包括指令和推理变体）达到70%-100%的攻击成功率（ASR）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着智能体AI的发展，函数调用型LLM被广泛用于调用外部函数扩展能力。现有研究已展示LLM易受用户提示操纵，而智能体模型的函数调用接口引入了新的安全漏洞，如数据篡改、窃取、死循环或生成有害内容。本文旨在揭示函数调用过程中工具选择环节的安全脆弱性。

Method: 提出函数劫持攻击（FHA），通过操控智能体模型的工具选择过程，强制调用攻击者指定的函数。FHA对上下文语义不敏感，对函数集具有鲁棒性，并可训练生成通用对抗函数，使其能在多种查询和负载配置下劫持工具选择。

Result: 在5种不同模型（包括指令变体和推理变体）上进行了实验，基于BFCL数据集，FHA达到70%至100%的攻击成功率（ASR）。

Conclusion: 智能体系统需要强大的防护措施和安全模块来防御此类攻击，FHA的发现表明函数调用接口的安全性亟待加强。

Abstract: The growth of agentic AI has drawn significant attention to function calling Large Language Models (LLMs), which are designed to extend the capabilities of AI-powered system by invoking external functions. Injection and jailbreaking attacks have been extensively explored to showcase the vulnerabilities of LLMs to user prompt manipulation. The expanded capabilities of agentic models introduce further vulnerabilities via their function calling interface. Recent work in LLM security showed that function calling can be abused, leading to data tampering and theft, causing disruptive behavior such as endless loops, or causing LLMs to produce harmful content in the style of jailbreaking attacks. This paper introduces a novel function hijacking attack (FHA) that manipulates the tool selection process of agentic models to force the invocation of a specific, attacker-chosen function. While existing attacks focus on semantic preference of the model for function-calling tasks, we show that FHA is largely agnostic to the context semantics and robust to the function sets, making it applicable across diverse domains. We further demonstrate that FHA can be trained to produce universal adversarial functions, enabling a single attacked function to hijack tool selection across multiple queries and payload configurations. We conducted experiments on 5 different models, including instructed and reasoning variants, reaching 70% to 100% ASR over the established BFCL dataset. Our findings further demonstrate the need for strong guardrails and security modules for agentic systems.

</details>


### [62] [VRSafe: A Secure Virtual Keyboard to Mitigate Keystroke Inference in Virtual Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21001)
*Yijun Yuan,Na Du,Adam J. Lee,Balaji Palanisamy*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为VRSafe的新型虚拟现实QWERTY键盘，通过引入假阳性击键和恶意登录检测器，有效抵御VR环境中的击键推断攻击，并在安全性和可用性之间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VR击键推断攻击研究较多，但缺乏有效且成本低廉的防御策略来缓解VR环境中的击键推断攻击。

Method: 设计了一种名为VRSafe的QWERTY键盘，通过在攻击者收集的键入信息中精心引入假阳性击键，使原始密码难以被推断；同时集成了恶意登录检测器，能够高效识别基于击键推断攻击获取凭证的未授权登录尝试。

Result: 通过仿真实验和真实用户研究评估，VRSafe能显著降低击键推断攻击的准确率，同时仅带来适度的可用性开销，且恶意登录检测器具有高检测率和极低的时间与内存成本。

Conclusion: VRSafe是一种有效的VR击键推断攻击防御方案，在保证安全性的同时兼顾了用户体验和系统资源开销。

Abstract: Password-based authentication is one of the most commonly used methods for verifying user identities, and its widespread usage continues in virtual reality (VR) applications. As a result, various forms of attacks on password-based authentication in traditional environments such as keystroke inference and shoulder surfing, are still effective in VR applications. While keystroke inference attacks on virtual keyboards have been studied extensively, few efforts have developed an effective and cost-efficient defense strategy to mitigate keystroke inferences in VR. To address this gap, this paper presents a novel QWERTY keyboard called \textit{VRSafe} that is resilient to keystroke inference attacks. The proposed keyboard carefully introduces false positive keystrokes into the information collected by attackers during the typing process, making the inference of the original password difficult. \textit{VRSafe} also incorporates a novel malicious login detector that can effectively identify unauthorized login attempts using credentials inferred from keystroke inference attacks with high detection rate and minimal time and memory cost. The proposed design is evaluated through both simulation experiments and a real-world user study, and the results show that \textit{VRSafe} can significantly reduce the accuracy of keystroke inference attacks while incurring a modest overhead from a usability standpoint.

</details>


### [63] [Layer 2 Blockchains Simplified: A Survey of Vector Commitment Schemes, ZKP Frameworks, Layer-2 Data Structures and Verkle Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21055)
*Ekleen Kaur,Marko Suvajdzic*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次提出了统一且聚焦安全的Layer-2（L2）协议综述，系统性地将L2架构映射到底层密码学安全性，形式化了包含排序器活性、桥接攻击和数据可用性失败的全面威胁模型，并分析了ZK框架（Groth16、Plonk）及其承诺方案（KZG、IPA）的安全假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: L2协议通过将计算卸载到链下并锚定信任到Layer-1，提升了吞吐量，但也引入了由排序器、桥接和数据可用性机制等链下组件构成的复杂安全面。现有文献[31][33]对此风险提供了零散的视角，缺乏统一的、以安全为中心的系统性综述。

Method: 本文采用结构化分析方法：从L1密码学原语出发，剖析到现代L2核心的技术演进；分析了ZK框架（Groth16、Plonk）的安全假设（离散对数、计算性Diffie-Hellman、双线性Diffie-Hellman）及对应承诺方案（KZG、IPA）；并形式化了涵盖排序器活性、桥接利用和数据可用性失败的全面L2威胁模型。

Result: 呈现了首个统一的、以安全为中心的L2协议综述，建立了L2架构与底层密码学安全之间的严格映射，并提供了完整的威胁模型分类。

Conclusion: 本文为研究人员和开发者提供了一个从深度密码数学视角推理L2安全性的可访问且严谨的参考，有助于系统性地理解和评估L2协议的安全基础。

Abstract: Layer-2 (L2) protocols address the fundamental limitations of Layer-1 (L1) blockchains by offloading computation while anchoring trust to the parent chain. This architectural shift, while boosting throughput, introduces a new, complex security surface defined by off-chain components like sequencers, bridges, and data availability mechanisms. Prior literature[31][33] offers fragmented views of this risk. This paper presents the first unified, security-focused survey that rigorously maps L2 architecture to its underlying cryptographic security. We dissect the technical progression from L1 primitives to the core of modern L2s, analyzing the security assumptions(Discrete Logarithm, Computational Diffie-Hellman, Bilinear Diffie-Hellman) of ZK frameworks (Groth16, Plonk) and their corresponding commitment schemes (KZG, IPA). We formalize a comprehensive L2 threat model encompassing sequencer liveness, bridge exploits, and data-availability failures. This work serves as an accessible yet rigorous reference for researchers and developers to reason about L2 security from a deep crypto-mathematical perspective.

</details>


### [64] [Behavioral Consistency and Transparency Analysis on Large Language Model API Gateways](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21083)
*Guanjie Lin,Yinxin Wan,Shichao Pei,Ting Xu,Kuai Xu,Guoliang Xue*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出GateScope，一个轻量级黑盒测量框架，用于评估商业LLM API网关的行为一致性和操作透明度。通过对10个真实商用网关的测量，发现了模型静默替换、响应截断、计费不准确和延迟不稳定等普遍问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第三方LLM API网关作为多厂商模型的统一接入点正在快速兴起，但其内部路由、缓存和计费策略缺乏透明度，用户无法验证请求是否由广告宣称的模型服务、响应是否忠实于上游API、账单是否准确反映公开定价。

Method: GateScope从四个关键维度审计网关：响应内容分析、多轮对话性能、计费准确性和延迟特征，通过黑盒方式检测模型降级/切换、静默截断、计费不准确和延迟不稳定等行为。

Result: 对10个真实商业LLM API网关的测量揭示了预期行为与实际行为之间的频繁差距，包括静默模型替换、记忆保持能力下降、偏离公开定价以及各平台间延迟稳定性的显著差异。

Conclusion: 商业LLM API网关普遍存在行为一致性和透明度不足的问题，GateScope能够有效检测这些行为偏差，为网关使用者提供可见性保障。

Abstract: Third-party Large Language Model (LLM) API gateways are rapidly emerging as unified access points to models offered by multiple vendors. However, the internal routing, caching, and billing policies of these gateways are largely undisclosed, leaving users with limited visibility into whether requests are served by the advertised models, whether responses remain faithful to upstream APIs, or whether invoices accurately reflect public pricing policies. To address this gap, we introduce GateScope, a lightweight black-box measurement framework for evaluating behavioral consistency and operational transparency in commercial LLM gateways. GateScope is designed to detect key misbehaviors, including model downgrading or switching, silent truncation, billing inaccuracies, and instability in latency by auditing gateways along four critical dimensions: response content analysis, multi-turn conversation performance, billing accuracy, and latency characteristics. Our measurements across 10 real-world commercial LLM API gateways reveal frequent gaps between expected and actual behaviors, including silent model substitutions, degraded memory retention, deviations from announced pricing, and substantial variation in latency stability across platforms.

</details>


### [65] [Cross-Session Threats in AI Agents: Benchmark, Evaluation, and Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21131)
*Ari Azarafrooz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出跨会话威胁检测问题，贡献了CSTM-Bench数据集（26种攻击分类、54个场景）、测量发现会话级检测器在跨会话攻击中召回率下降约50%，以及提出基于核心集记忆的Coreset Memory Reader算法和CSR_prefix评估指标，实现跨会话威胁检测的召回率与系统稳定性平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI智能体护栏是无记忆的，每个消息被孤立判断，导致攻击者将单个攻击分散到数十个会话中即可绕过所有会话级检测器，因为只有聚合后才构成有效载荷。亟需跨会话威胁检测方法。

Method: (1) 构建CSTM-Bench数据集，包含26种可执行攻击分类，按杀伤链阶段和跨会话操作分类，绑定身份锚点，含良性混淆样本；(2) 将跨会话检测建模为信息瓶颈问题，对比会话级检测器与全日志关联器；(3) 提出Coreset Memory Reader（K=50有界记忆核心集），保留最高信号片段，并引入CSR_prefix指标（有序前缀稳定性），融合为CSTM综合指标。

Result: 会话级检测器和全日志关联器在dilution到cross_session分片时，攻击召回率均下降约50%。Coreset Memory Reader是唯一在两个分片上召回率均保持稳定的方法。

Conclusion: 跨会话威胁检测需要带记忆的检测架构，Coreset Memory Reader方案有效，CSR_prefix指标可评估部署稳定性，CSTM指标提供召回率与稳定性的帕累托前沿基准。

Abstract: AI-agent guardrails are memoryless: each message is judged in isolation, so an adversary who spreads a single attack across dozens of sessions slips past every session-bound detector because only the aggregate carries the payload. We make three contributions to cross-session threat detection.
  (1) Dataset. CSTM-Bench is 26 executable attack taxonomies classified by kill-chain stage and cross-session operation (accumulate, compose, launder, inject_on_reader), each bound to one of seven identity anchors that ground-truth "violation" as a policy predicate, plus matched Benign-pristine and Benign-hard confounders. Released on Hugging Face as intrinsec-ai/cstm-bench with two 54-scenario splits: dilution (compositional) and cross_session (12 isolation-invisible scenarios produced by a closed-loop rewriter that softens surface phrasing while preserving cross-session artefacts).
  (2) Measurement. Framing cross-session detection as an information bottleneck to a downstream correlator LLM, we find that a session-bound judge and a Full-Log Correlator concatenating every prompt into one long-context call both lose roughly half their attack recall moving from dilution to cross_session, well inside any frontier context window. Scope: 54 scenarios per shard, one correlator family (Anthropic Claude), no prompt optimisation; we release it to motivate larger, multi-provider datasets.
  (3) Algorithm and metric. A bounded-memory Coreset Memory Reader retaining highest-signal fragments at $K=50$ is the only reader whose recall survives both shards. Because ranker reshuffles break KV-cache prefix reuse, we promote $\mathrm{CSR\_prefix}$ (ordered prefix stability, LLM-free) to a first-class metric and fuse it with detection into $\mathrm{CSTM} = 0.7 F_1(\mathrm{CSDA@action}, \mathrm{precision}) + 0.3 \mathrm{CSR\_prefix}$, benchmarking rankers on a single Pareto of recall versus serving stability.

</details>


### [66] [Position Paper: Denial-of-Service Against Multi-Round Transaction Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21169)
*Yuzhe Tang,Yibo Wang,Wanning Ding,Jiaqi Chen,Taesoo Kim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了针对以太坊打包服务（如Flashbots Bundles）的新型非对称拒绝服务攻击，通过利用交易间依赖关系和打包服务特性，实现了高成功率的低成本攻击，并提出了相应缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拒绝服务攻击设计由于无法适应打包服务独特的多轮执行模型而效果不佳，因此需要研究专门针对打包服务的非对称DoS攻击方法。

Method: 利用合约状态的交易间依赖关系实现攻击的隐蔽性，并利用原子区块包含等打包服务特有功能来降低资金和操作成本。

Result: 实验表明该攻击成功率高，能显著降低构建者收入并减缓区块生产速度。

Conclusion: 证明了打包服务存在安全漏洞，并提出了相应的缓解策略。

Abstract: In Ethereum, transaction-bundling services are a critical component of block builders, such as Flashbots Bundles, and are widely used by MEV searchers. Disrupting bundling services can degrade searcher experience and reduce builder revenue. Despite the extensive studies, the existing denial-of-service attack designs are ineffective against bundling services due to their unique multi-round execution model.
  This paper studies the open problem of asymmetric denial-of-service against bundling services. We develop evasive, risk-free, and low-cost DoS attacks on Flashbots' bundling service, the only open-source bundling service known to us. Our attacks exploit inter-transaction dependencies through contract state to achieve evasiveness, and abuse bundling-specific features, such as atomic block inclusion, to significantly reduce both capital and operational costs of the attack.
  Experimental results show that our attacks achieve high success rates, substantially reduce builders' revenue, and slow block production. We further propose mitigation strategies for the identified risks.

</details>


### [67] [Physically Unclonable Functions for Secure IoT Authentication and Hardware-Anchored AI Model Integrity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21188)
*Maryam Taghi Zadeh,Mohsen Ahmadi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了AI驱动的IoT与边缘计算系统中硬件根信任机制，比较了TPM、PUF、混合容器感知信任锚及纯软件方案，指出硬件根方案在抗物理篡改和克隆方面优于纯软件方案，PUF和混合信任锚在大规模AIoT系统中具有最佳平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI技术与IoT及边缘计算系统的快速融合对设备认证和AI模型完整性提出了更高的安全和资源约束要求，亟需硬件根信任机制来保障系统可信性。

Method: 采用系统性文献综述方法，对TPM测量与认证、硅基和FPGA基物理不可克隆函数(PUF)、混合容器感知硬件信任根以及纯软件安全方法等代表性信任锚机制进行系统比较和分析。

Result: 硬件根方案在对抗性及物理暴露环境中提供更强的抗物理篡改和设备克隆防护；PUF和混合信任锚在大规模AIoT系统中实现了安全性、可扩展性、成本和部署复杂度的最佳平衡；纯软件信任机制在对抗性物理暴露场景中仍显不足。

Conclusion: PUF和混合信任锚为大规模AIoT系统提供了有前景的信任方案，研究结果为可信AIoT平台的设计挑战提供了清晰认识，并为未来发展方向提供了指导。

Abstract: The rapid integration of artificial intelligence (AI) into Internet of Things (IoT) and edge computing systems has intensified the need for robust, hardware-rooted trust mechanisms capable of ensuring device authenticity and AI model integrity under strict resource and security constraints. This survey reviews and synthesizes existing literature on hardware-rooted trust mechanisms for AI-enabled IoT systems. It systematically examines and compares representative trust anchor mechanisms, including Trusted Platform Module (TPM)-based measurement and attestation, silicon and FPGA-based Physical Unclonable Functions (PUFs), hybrid container-aware hardware roots of trust, and software-only security approaches. The analysis highlights how hardware-rooted solutions generally provide stronger protection against physical tampering and device cloning compared to software-only approaches, particularly in adversarial and physically exposed environments, while hybrid designs extend hardware trust into runtime and containerized environments commonly used in modern edge deployments. By evaluating trade-offs among security strength, scalability, cost, and deployment complexity, the study shows that PUF-based and hybrid trust anchors offer a promising balance for large-scale, AI-enabled IoT systems, whereas software-only trust mechanisms remain insufficient in adversarial and physically exposed settings. The presented comparison aims to clarify current design challenges and guide future development of trustworthy AI-enabled IoT platforms.

</details>


### [68] [ECCFROG522PP: An Enhanced 522 bit Weierstrass Elliptic Curve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21261)
*Victor Duarte Melo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出ECCFROG522PP，一种522位素域椭圆曲线，所有参数通过BLAKE3确定性管道从公开种子生成，强调透明性、可审计性和可复现性，目标安全级别与P-521相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有椭圆曲线（如NIST P-256、secp256k1）提供约128位经典安全，更高安全需求的曲线（如NIST P-521、Curve448、Brainpool P512）缺乏完全透明且可复现的参数生成流程。作者旨在设计一条参数生成过程完全公开、可独立验证的椭圆曲线，以增强密码系统的审计可信度。

Method: 采用BLAKE3哈希函数构建确定性参数生成管道，所有关键参数（曲线系数、基点、阶等）均由固定公开种子通过指定索引计算得出；执行标准安全性检查（低嵌入度约减、CM判别式异常等），并计算二次扭转的大素因子下界及嵌入度下界。

Result: 生成的曲线具有素阶、协因子为1、基点为满阶；二次扭转存在大素因子，嵌入度有可证明下界；所有参数和检查均可通过公开脚本端到端复现。

Conclusion: ECCFROG522PP提供了一种与P-521同安全级别但生成过程完全透明可审计的椭圆曲线方案，适合对参数可信度有严格要求的应用场景。

Abstract: This paper presents ECCFROG522PP, a 522-bit prime-field elliptic curve in short Weierstrass form, designed with a focus on deterministic generation and public reproducibility. The central design principle is that all critical parameters are derived from a fixed public seed through a transparent and verifiable procedure. While many deployed systems rely on NIST P-256 and secp256k1, which target approximately 128-bit classical security, higher security applications typically consider curves such as NIST P-521, Curve448, and Brainpool P512. ECCFROG522PP is intended for the same general classical security range as P-521, with emphasis on transparency, auditability, and reproducibility rather than performance optimization.
  The curve parameters are generated through a BLAKE3-based deterministic pipeline with publicly specified indices. The resulting construction has prime order, cofactor one, and a deterministically derived base point of full order. The quadratic twist has a large proven prime factor, and the construction includes a documented lower bound on the embedding degree together with standard sanity checks against low embedding degree reductions and basic CM discriminant anomalies.
  The full generation and validation procedure can be reproduced end to end from public artifacts and reference scripts, enabling independent verification of all parameters and checks.

</details>


### [69] [Adversarial Evasion in Non-Stationary Malware Detection: Minimizing Drift Signals through Similarity-Constrained Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21310)
*Pawan Acharya,Lan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究在非平稳环境下生成既能逃避分类又能规避漂移监测的对抗性恶意软件样本，通过相似性正则化约束优化目标，实验表明ℓ2正则化在减少输出漂移信号方面效果最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习方法在恶意软件检测中表现出色，但在现实非平稳环境中面临分类模型和检测系统持续演化的关键限制，需探究攻击者能否同时逃避分类和漂移监测。

Method: 在分类器的标准化特征空间中生成定向对抗样本，融入相似性正则化项，通过约束扰动保持与干净恶意软件的分布相似性，构建平衡定向误分类与漂移信号最小化的优化目标。

Result: 相似性约束可有效降低输出漂移信号，ℓ2正则化表现最佳；扰动预算显著影响逃避-可检测性权衡，预算越大攻击成功率越高但漂移指标越明显。

Conclusion: 提出了一种兼顾逃避分类与规避漂移监测的对抗攻击框架，揭示了扰动预算在攻击效能与隐蔽性之间的关键权衡作用。

Abstract: Deep learning has emerged as a powerful approach for malware detection, demonstrating impressive accuracy across various data representations. However, these models face critical limitations in real-world, non-stationary environments where both malware characteristics and detection systems continuously evolve. Our research investigates a fundamental security question: Can an attacker generate adversarial malware samples that simultaneously evade classification and remain inconspicuous to drift monitoring mechanisms? We propose a novel approach that generates targeted adversarial examples in the classifier's standardized feature space, augmented with sophisticated similarity regularizers. By carefully constraining perturbations to maintain distributional similarity with clean malware, we create an optimization objective that balances targeted misclassification with drift signal minimization. We quantify the effectiveness of this approach by comprehensively comparing classifier output probabilities using multiple drift metrics. Our experiments demonstrate that similarity constraints can reduce output drift signals, with $\ell_2$ regularization showing the most promising results. We observe that perturbation budget significantly influences the evasion-detectability trade-off, with increased budget leading to higher attack success rates and more substantial drift indicators.

</details>


### [70] [Provably Secure Steganography Based on List Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21394)
*Kaiyi Pang,Minhao Bai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于列表解码的可证明安全隐写方案，通过维护包含正确秘密消息的候选集合并引入后缀匹配机制，显著提高了嵌入容量，同时在三种大语言模型和七个基线上的实验验证了其计算效率与容量优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于语言模型的可证明安全隐写方案（PSS）虽能保证计算不可区分性，但嵌入容量不足，尤其不适用于大语言模型（LLMs）固有的低熵倾向，导致可行嵌入容量严重受限。

Method: 提出基于列表解码的隐写方案：维护包含正确秘密消息的候选集合而非直接寻找正确消息，充分挖掘生成文本的信息内容；引入后缀匹配机制从候选中区分正确秘密消息；提供安全性和正确性的理论证明，并推导理论容量下界。该方案即插即用，仅需替换模型的标准随机采样模块。

Result: 在三类LLMs和七个PSS基线上的实验表明，该方法在保持与先前PSS方案相当的计算效率的同时，显著提升了嵌入容量。

Conclusion: 提出了一种理论上证明高容量的可证明安全隐写方案，通过列表解码策略有效提升了LLMs场景下的嵌入容量，且具备即插即用的实用优势。

Abstract: Steganography embeds secret messages in seemingly innocuous carriers for covert communication under surveillance. Current Provably Secure Steganography (PSS) schemes based on language models can guarantee computational indistinguishability between the covertext and stegotext. However, achieving high embedding capacity remains a challenge for existing PSS. The inefficient entropy utilization renders them not well-suited for Large Language Models (LLMs), whose inherent low-entropy tendencies severely constrain feasible embedding capacity. To address this, we propose a provably secure steganography scheme with a theoretically proved high capacity. Our scheme is based on the concept of list decoding: it maintains a set of candidates that contain the correct secret message, instead of directly finding the correct message with more effort. This strategy fully utilizes the information content of the generated text, yielding higher capacity. To ensure the correctness of our scheme, we further introduce a suffix-matching mechanism to distinguish the correct secret message from the candidates. We provide theoretical proofs for both the security and correctness of our scheme, alongside a derivation of its theoretical capacity lower bound.
  Our approach is plug-and-play, requiring only a direct replacement of the model's standard random sampling module. Experiments on three LLMs and seven PSS baselines demonstrate that our method achieves computational efficiency comparable to prior PSS schemes while delivering a substantial improvement in embedding capacity.

</details>


### [71] [CSC: Turning the Adversary's Poison against Itself](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21416)
*Yuchen Shi,Xin Guo,Huajie Chen,Tianqing Zhu,Bo Liu,Wanlei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Cluster Segregation Concealment (CSC)防御方法，通过早期特征聚类分离中毒样本并重标为虚拟类，在四个基准数据集上对十二种投毒攻击实现了接近零的攻击成功率，同时保持模型对干净数据的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于毒害抑制的防御方法存在对特定攻击变种检测不足的问题，且通过遗忘机制会损害模型效用导致精度下降，亟需一种既能有效抑制后门攻击又不牺牲模型准确性的防御方法。

Method: CSC方法包含两个阶段：1) 分离阶段：在标准监督学习早期epoch进行特征提取，使用DBSCAN聚类，基于类多样性和密度指标识别异常聚类（中毒样本）；2) 隐藏阶段：将识别的中毒样本重标为虚拟类，通过交叉熵损失微调分类器，用良性虚拟关联替换后门关联。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上对十二种投毒攻击进行评估，CSC优于九种最先进防御方法，将平均攻击成功率降至接近零，且干净精度损失极小。

Conclusion: CSC通过早期识别中毒样本并重新标记为虚拟类，有效抑制后门攻击而不牺牲模型效用，为可信人工智能提供了有力支持。

Abstract: Poisoning-based backdoor attacks pose significant threats to deep neural networks by embedding triggers in training data, causing models to misclassify triggered inputs as adversary-specified labels while maintaining performance on clean data. Existing poison restraint-based defenses often suffer from inadequate detection against specific attack variants and compromise model utility through unlearning methods that lead to accuracy degradation. This paper conducts a comprehensive analysis of backdoor attack dynamics during model training, revealing that poisoned samples form isolated clusters in latent space early on, with triggers acting as dominant features distinct from benign ones. Leveraging these insights, we propose Cluster Segregation Concealment (CSC), a novel poison suppression defense. CSC first trains a deep neural network via standard supervised learning while segregating poisoned samples through feature extraction from early epochs, DBSCAN clustering, and identification of anomalous clusters based on class diversity and density metrics. In the concealment stage, identified poisoned samples are relabeled to a virtual class, and the model's classifier is fine-tuned using cross-entropy loss to replace the backdoor association with a benign virtual linkage, preserving overall accuracy. CSC was evaluated on four benchmark datasets against twelve poisoning-based attacks, CSC outperforms nine state-of-the-art defenses by reducing average attack success rates to near zero with minimal clean accuracy loss. Contributions include robust backdoor patterns identification, an effective concealment mechanism, and superior empirical validation, advancing trustworthy artificial intelligence.

</details>


### [72] [Differentially Private De-identification of Dutch Clinical Notes: A Comparative Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21421)
*Michele Miranda,Xinlan Yan,Nishant Mishra,Rachel Murphy,Ameen Abu-Hanna,Sébastien Bratières,Iacer Calixto*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次比较了差分隐私(DP)、命名实体识别(NER)和大语言模型(LLM)在荷兰语临床文本去标识化中的表现，发现单独使用DP会严重损害效用，但与语言预处理（尤其是LLM）结合能显著改善隐私-效用权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 保护临床病历中的患者隐私对于在GDPR和HIPAA等法规下实现医疗数据的二次利用至关重要。手动去标识化虽是最佳标准但成本高、速度慢，亟需既保证隐私又保持高可用性的自动化方法。

Method: 对DP、NER和LLM三种方法进行单独评估，并探索NER或LLM预处理后接DP的混合策略，通过隐私泄漏度和外部评估（实体与关系分类）衡量性能。

Result: 单独使用DP机制会显著降低数据效用；结合语言预处理（尤其是基于LLM的遮盖）能有效改善隐私-效用权衡。

Conclusion: 混合策略，特别是LLM预处理后接DP，是荷兰语临床文本去标识化的有效方案，实现了更好的隐私保护与数据效用平衡。

Abstract: Protecting patient privacy in clinical narratives is essential for enabling secondary use of healthcare data under regulations such as GDPR and HIPAA. While manual de-identification remains the gold standard, it is costly and slow, motivating the need for automated methods that combine privacy guarantees with high utility. Most automated text de-identification pipelines employed named entity recognition (NER) to identify protected entities for redaction. Although methods based on differential privacy (DP) provide formal privacy guarantees, more recently also large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for text de-identification in the clinical domain. In this work, we present the first comparative study of DP, NER, and LLMs for Dutch clinical text de-identification. We investigate these methods separately as well as hybrid strategies that apply NER or LLM preprocessing prior to DP, and assess performance in terms of privacy leakage and extrinsic evaluation (entity and relation classification). We show that DP mechanisms alone degrade utility substantially, but combining them with linguistic preprocessing, especially LLM-based redaction, significantly improves the privacy-utility trade-off.

</details>


### [73] [A Stackelberg Model for Hybridization in Cryptography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21436)
*Willie Kouam,Stefan Rass,Zahra Seyedi,Shahzad Ahmad,Eckhard Pfluegel*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文将密码学中的混合加密策略建模为Stackelberg博弈：防御方在经典、后量子或混合算法中选择，攻击方观察到选择后选用合适的密码分析方法。防御方优化问题被转化为线性规划，攻击方子问题则用动态规划求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用博弈论框架分析密码学中防御方与攻击方之间的策略互动，特别是混合加密策略在安全性与成本之间的权衡问题。

Method: 将问题建模为资源约束下的Stackelberg密码混合博弈：防御方随机化选择加密算法，攻击方观测后条件优化选择密码分析方法；攻击方子问题用动态规划求解，防御方主问题转化为线性规划。

Result: 提出了攻击方子问题的动态规划方法以及防御方Stackelberg优化的线性规划形式。

Conclusion: 该博弈论框架为混合加密策略在资源受限条件下的最优选择提供了理论建模与求解方法。

Abstract: Similar to a strategic interaction between rational and intelligent agents, cryptography problems can be examined through the prism of game theory. In this setting, the agent aiming to protect a message is called the defender, while the one attempting to decrypt it, generally for malicious purposes, is the attacker. To strengthen security in cryptography, various strategies have been developed, among which hybridization stands out as a key concept in modern cryptographic design. This strategy allows the defender to select among different encryption algorithms (classical, post-quantum, or hybrid) while carefully balancing security and operational costs. On the other side, the attacker, limited by available resources, chooses cryptanalysis methods capable of breaching the selected algorithm. We model this interaction as a Stackelberg cryptographic hybridization problem under resource constraints. Here, the defender randomizes over encryption algorithms, and the attacker observes the choice before selecting suitable cryptanalysis methods. The attacker's decision is framed as a conditional optimization problem, which we refer to as the ``attacker subgame''. We then propose a dynamic programming approach for the attacker's subgame, while the defender's Stackelberg optimization is formulated as a linear program.

</details>


### [74] [Benchmarking the Utility of Privacy-Preserving Cox Regression Under Data-Driven Clipping Bounds: A Multi-Dataset Simulation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21491)
*Keita Fukuyama,Yukiko Mori,Tomohiro Kuroda,Hiroaki Kikuchi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究系统评估了差分隐私机制（拉普拉斯机制与随机响应）对Cox比例风险模型统计效用的影响。使用5个临床数据集、15个ε水平（0.1-1000）和1000次蒙特卡洛迭代，比较了三种输入扰动与输出扰动方法。结果表明：在标准DP水平（ε≤1）下，约90%的显著协变量失去显著性；输入扰动中仅扰动协变量效果最佳；实际应用中需要ε≥10（预测性能）至ε≥30-60（显著性保持）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差分隐私虽能保证个体隐私，但其对生存分析中统计效用的系统性影响评估仍然缺乏，本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用拉普拉斯机制和随机响应两种DP机制，结合数据驱动的裁剪边界（观测min/max，不提供正式ε-DP保证），在5个临床数据集（n=168-6,524）、15个ε水平（0.1-1000）和B=1000次蒙特卡洛迭代下，对Cox比例风险模型进行系统评估。比较三种输入扰动（仅协变量、所有输入、离散时间模型）和输出扰动（基于dfbeta的敏感性），以显著性丢失率（LSR）、C-index和系数偏差为评估指标。

Result: 在ε≤1时，约90%-94%的显著协变量失去显著性，预测性能接近随机水平。仅扰动协变量的输入扰动方法效果最佳；输出扰动在ε≥5时保持接近基线性能。在n≈3000时，显著性在ε=3-10区间快速恢复。实际需要ε≥10（预测性能）至ε≥30-60（显著性保持）。在中等至高ε范围，基线p值接近显著性阈值的变量出现假阳性率增加。
